\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\\
\lesssim\|\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Proof.

Let
f(t)=U^{\varepsilon}(t)f_{0}
for all
t\geq 0
which satisfies (
3.9
), so that
\widehat{f}(t,\xi)=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)
satisfies (
3.10
) for all
\xi\in\mathbf{R}^{3}
.
Using Proposition
3.1
we have, for all
t\geq 0
and some
\lambda_{0}>0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda_{0}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right),

and we already observe that, using
\|\cdot\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\geq\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\cdot\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\geq\|\cdot\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}
and
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
,

\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\gtrsim\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2},

where we have used that
|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}
is equivalent to
\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}
independently of
\xi
and
\varepsilon
. Therefore it follows

(3.19)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-2\lambda\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right),

for some constants
\lambda,\sigma>0
.
We now split our analysis into two cases: high frequencies
|\xi|\geq 1
and low frequencies
|\xi|<1
.

For high frequencies
|\xi|\geq 1
we remark that
\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\geq\frac{1}{2}
, hence we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{\sigma}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right).

Arguing as in the proof of Proposition
3.4
we hence deduce

(3.20)

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

We now investigate the case of low frequencies
|\xi|<1
. We denote by
p^{\prime}
the conjugate exponent of
p
, that is
1/p+1/p^{\prime}=1
with the convention
p^{\prime}=1
if
p=\infty
, and consider a real number
r
verifying
1+p^{\prime}/3<r<1+1/(2\vartheta)
, which we observe is possible thanks to the conditions on
p
and
\vartheta
.
Remarking that
|\xi|^{2}\leq 2|\xi|^{2}/\langle\xi\rangle^{2}
if
|\xi|<1
, by Young’s inequality we get: for any
\delta>0
there is
C_{\delta}>0
such that, for all
|\xi|<1
and
t\geq 0
, we have

(3.21)

1\leq\delta(1+t)\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}+C_{\delta}(1+t)^{-\frac{1}{r-1}}|\xi|^{-\frac{2}{r-1}}.

We therefore obtain, coming back to (
3.19
) and choosing
\delta>0
appropriately,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\vartheta(1+t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{-1-\frac{1}{r-1}}|\xi|^{-\frac{2}{r-1}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}.

for some constant
C>0
. Multiplying both sides by
(1+t)^{2\vartheta}
gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{(1+t)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq-\sigma(1+t)^{2\vartheta}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{2\vartheta-1-\frac{1}{r-1}}|\xi|^{-\frac{2}{r-1}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}.

Integrating in time implies, for all
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle(1+t)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\tau,\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\tau,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{2}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2},

where we have used that
(1+t)^{2\vartheta-1-\frac{1}{r-1}}
is integrable since
r<1+1/(2\vartheta)
.
We now take the supremum in time and finally the square-root of the resulting estimate, which gives

(3.22)

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Gathering the estimate for high frequencies (
3.20
) together with the one for low frequencies (
3.22
), it follows

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}
norm above, we use Hölder’s inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\,\mathrm{d}\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{p^{\prime}}{r-1}}\,\mathrm{d}\xi\right)^{1/p^{\prime}}\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

since
r>1+p^{\prime}/3
, which implies

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

and concludes the proof.
∎

Proposition 3.9
.

Let
\ell\geq 0
,
p\in(3/2,\infty]
and
0<\vartheta<\frac{3}{2}(1-\frac{1}{p})
.
Let
S=S(t,x,v)
verify
\mathbf{P}S=0
and
\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{S}\in L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}
, and denote

g_{S}(t)=\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)S(\tau)\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Assume that
\widehat{g}_{S}\in L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})
, then

\displaystyle\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{g}_{S}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{g}_{S}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{g}_{S}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{S}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\left\|\widehat{g}_{S}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Proof.

Recalling that
\widehat{g}_{S}
satisfies (
3.12
), we can argue as for obtaining (
3.19
) to get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-2\lambda\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{S}(\xi)\|_{(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}^{2},

for some constants
\lambda,\sigma,C>0
. By separating the cases of high and low frequencies, we can conclude exactly as in the proof of Proposition
3.8
.
∎

3.5.
Decay estimates: Soft potentials in the whole space

In this subsection we shall always assume
\gamma+2s<0
and
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
, and we shall obtain decay estimates for the semigroup
U^{\varepsilon}
(see Proposition
3.10
) as well as its integral in time against a source
\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)S(\tau)\,\mathrm{d}\tau
(see Proposition
3.11
). We recall that given any real number
\omega\in\mathbf{R}
we denote
\mathrm{p}_{\omega}:t\mapsto(1+t)^{\omega}
.

Proposition 3.10
.

Let
p\in(3/2,\infty]
and
0<\vartheta<\frac{3}{2}(1-\frac{1}{p})
. Let
f_{0}\in\mathcal{F}_{x}^{-1}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})\cap L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v})
with
\ell>\vartheta|\gamma+2s|
, then we have

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}(\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0})\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Proof.

Arguing as in the proof of Proposition
3.8
, denoting
f(t)=U^{\varepsilon}(t)f_{0}
and using that
\|\cdot\|_{H^{s,*}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\geq\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\cdot\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}
, we first obtain

(3.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}-\lambda\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right),

for some positive constants
\lambda,\sigma>0
. We now split the analysis into high frequencies and low frequencies.

For high frequencies
|\xi|\geq 1
we observe that
\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\geq\frac{1}{2}
, which yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}-\lambda\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right),

for some other constants
\lambda,\sigma>0
. Thanks to the interpolation inequality (
3.17
) of the proof of Proposition
3.6
, we hence deduce

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\omega(1+t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{-1-\frac{2\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2},

for any
\vartheta<\omega<\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}
and some constant
C>0
. With this inequality we can thus argue as in the proof of Proposition
3.6
, which gives, recalling that
(1+t)^{2\omega-1-\frac{2\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}
is integrable since
0<\omega<\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}
,

(3.24)

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

We now turn our attention to the low frequencies case
|\xi|<1
. First of all, from (
3.23
), we use the interpolation inequality (
3.17
) of the proof of Proposition
3.6
to deduce

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\omega(1+t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}-\lambda\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{-1-\frac{2\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2},

for any
\vartheta<\omega<\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}
and some constant
C>0
.
As in the proof of Proposition
3.8
, we denote by
p^{\prime}
the conjugate exponent of
p
, and consider a real number
r
verifying
1+p^{\prime}/3<r<1+1/(2\vartheta)
. Using inequality (
3.21
) we hence deduce

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\vartheta(1+t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{-1-\frac{2\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{-1-\frac{1}{r-1}}|\xi|^{-\frac{2}{r-1}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2},

for some constant
C>0
. Multiplying both sides by
(1+t)^{2\vartheta}
gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{(1+t)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{f}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq-\sigma(1+t)^{2\vartheta}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{2\vartheta-1-\frac{2\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+t)^{2\vartheta-1-\frac{1}{r-1}}|\xi|^{-\frac{2}{r-1}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.

Integrating in time implies, for all
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle(1+t)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\tau,\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{2\vartheta}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\tau,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{2}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}^{2},

where we have used that
(1+t)^{2\vartheta-1-\frac{2\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}
and
(1+t)^{2\vartheta-1-\frac{1}{r-1}}
are integrable since
0<\vartheta<\omega<\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}
and
r<1+1/(2\vartheta)
, respectively.
We can now take the supremum in time and then the square-root of the resulting estimate, which gives

(3.25)

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Gathering the estimate for high frequencies (
3.24
) together with the one for low frequencies (
3.25
) and observing that
\vartheta<\omega
, it follows

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}(\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}_{0}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}
norm above, we use Hölder’s inequality to control the last term in the right-hand side as in the proof of Proposition
3.8
, to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{r-1}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\,\mathrm{d}\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

since
r>1+p^{\prime}/3
, which implies

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

and concludes the proof.
∎

Proposition 3.11
.

Let
p\in(3/2,\infty]
and
0<\vartheta<\frac{3}{2}(1-\frac{1}{p})
. Let
S=S(t,x,v)
verify
\mathbf{P}S=0
and
\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{S}\in L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}
, and denote

g_{S}(t)=\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)S(\tau)\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Assume that
g_{S}\in\mathcal{F}_{x}^{-1}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})\cap L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v})
with
\ell>\vartheta|\gamma+2s|
, then

\displaystyle\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{g}_{S}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{g}_{S}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{g}_{S}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{S}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\widehat{g}_{S}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{g}_{S}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Proof.

Recalling that
\widehat{g}_{S}
satisfies (
3.12
), we can argue as for obtaining (
3.23
) to get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)|\hskip-1.13791pt|\hskip-1.13791pt|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}-\lambda\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{g}_{S}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)+C\varepsilon^{2}\|\widehat{S}(\xi)\|_{(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}^{2},

for some constants
\lambda,\sigma,C>0
. By separating the cases of high and low frequencies, we can conclude exactly as in the proof of Proposition
3.10
.
∎

4.
Well-posedness and regularization for the rescaled Boltzmann equation

Consider the equation (
1.9
) that we rewrite here

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}f^{\varepsilon}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}(L-\varepsilon v\cdot\nabla_{x})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\\
&f^{\varepsilon}_{t=0}=f^{\varepsilon}_{0}.\end{aligned}\right.

We shall consider mild solutions of (
1.9
), that is, we shall prove the well-posedness of a solution
f^{\varepsilon}
to (
1.9
) in Duhamel’s form

(4.1)

f^{\varepsilon}(t)=U^{\varepsilon}(t)f^{\varepsilon}_{0}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Taking the Fourier transform in space of (
1.9
), we have

(4.2)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}(\xi)=\Lambda^{\varepsilon}(\xi)\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}(\xi)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})(\xi)\\
&\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}(\xi)_{t=0}=\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}(\xi),\end{aligned}\right.

and by Duhamel’s formula

(4.3)

\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}(\xi)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

4.1.
Nonlinear estimates

We start by recalling some well-known trilinear estimates on the collision operator
\Gamma
established in
[
44
,
1
,
5
]
.
We start with estimates without velocity weight.
From
[
44
,
5
]
, for the hard potentials case
\gamma+2s\geq 0
there holds

(4.4)

\left|\langle\Gamma(f,g),h\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right|\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\|h\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}.

Moreover from
[
1
]
, for the soft potentials case
\gamma+2s<0
one has

(4.5)

\displaystyle\left|\langle\Gamma(f,g),h\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}+\|f\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right)\|h\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{v}},\|f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\}\|h\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}.

From these estimates we already obtain

(4.6)

\displaystyle\|\Gamma(f,g)\|_{(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|\phi\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\leq 1}\langle\Gamma(f,g),\phi\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}+\|f\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{v}},\|f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\},

where we denote
a_{-}=-\min(-a,0)
, which holds for both hard and soft potentials.

For the soft potentials case, we shall also need estimates when adding velocity weight
\langle v\rangle^{\ell}
. From (
4.5
) together with the commutator estimate of
[
1
, Proposition 3.13]
, there holds

(4.7)

\displaystyle\left|\langle\Gamma(f,g),h\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|f\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right)\|h\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right\}\|h\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Therefore we also deduce

(4.8)

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\Gamma(f,g)\|_{(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|\phi\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\leq 1}\langle\Gamma(f,g),\phi\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|f\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|f\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}g\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right\},

for the soft potentials case.

Thanks to (
4.6
) we deduce our main nonlinear estimate without weight.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
p\in[1,\infty]
. For any smooth enough functions
f,g
there holds

\|\widehat{\Gamma}(f,g)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}\lesssim\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}+\min\left\{\Gamma_{3},\Gamma_{4}\right\}

where

\displaystyle\Gamma_{1}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\Big\},

\displaystyle\Gamma_{2}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\Big\},

\displaystyle\Gamma_{3}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\Big\},

and

\displaystyle\Gamma_{4}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\Big\}.

Proof.

Using (
4.6
) we write

\displaystyle\left\{\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f(t),g(t))(\xi)\|_{(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim I_{1}+I_{2}+\min\{I_{3},I_{4}\}

with

I_{1}=\left\{\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}f(t,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(t,\eta)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\right\}^{1/2},

I_{2}=\left\{\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|f(t,\xi-\eta)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}(t,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\right\}^{1/2},

I_{3}=\left\{\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}f(t,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(t,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\right\}^{1/2},

and

I_{4}=\left\{\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|f(t,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{g}(t,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\right\}^{1/2}.

We now investigate the term
I_{1}
. Thanks to Minkowski and Hölder inequalities we then obtain

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}(t,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\|\widehat{g}(t,\eta)\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\right)^{1/2}\mathrm{d}\eta

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta.

Taking the
L^{p}_{\xi}
norm in above estimate and using Young’s inequality for convolution we first obtain

I_{1}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\quad\text{and}\quad I_{1}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}.

Arguing exactly as above but exchanging the role of
f
and
g
when performing Hölder’s inequality, we also obtain

I_{1}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\quad\text{and}\quad I_{1}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},

that is
I_{1}\lesssim\Gamma_{1}
.

The estimates for the other terms
I_{2}
,
I_{3}
and
I_{4}
can be obtained exactly as for
I_{1}
, so we omit it.
∎

Arguing exactly as in the proof of Lemma
4.1
but using the weighted estimate (
4.8
), we also obtain the main weighted nonlinear estimate for soft potentials below, the proof of which we omit for simplicity.

Lemma 4.2
.

Let
\ell>0
,
\gamma+2s<0
and
p\in[1,\infty]
. For any smooth enough functions
f,g
there holds

\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f,g)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}\lesssim\widetilde{\Gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\Gamma}_{2}+\min\left\{\widetilde{\Gamma}_{3},\widetilde{\Gamma}_{4}\right\}

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Gamma}_{1}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\Big\},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Gamma}_{2}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\Big\},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Gamma}_{3}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\Big\},

and

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Gamma}_{4}=\min\Big\{

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma/2+s}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\Big\}.

4.2.
Proof of Theorem
2.1
–(1)

We consider the torus case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
.

4.2.1.
Global existence

Let
\ell=0
in the hard potentials case
\gamma+2s\geq 0
, and
\ell\geq 0
in the soft potentials case
\gamma+2s<0
. We define the space

\mathscr{X}=\left\{f\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})\cap L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}))\mid f\text{ satisfies }\eqref{eq:normalization2},\;\|f\|_{\mathscr{X}}<\infty\right\}

with

\|f\|_{\mathscr{X}}:=\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Let
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}))
verify

\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\leq\eta_{0},

and consider the map
\Phi:\mathscr{X}\to\mathscr{X}
,
f^{\varepsilon}\mapsto\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]
defined by, for all
t\geq 0
,

(4.9)

\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}](t)=U^{\varepsilon}(t)f^{\varepsilon}_{0}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

thus, for all
\xi\in\mathbf{Z}^{3}
,

(4.10)

\widehat{\Phi}[f^{\varepsilon}](t,\xi)=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}(\xi)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Thanks to Proposition
3.2
we deduce, for some constant
C_{0}>0
independent of
\varepsilon
, that

\|U^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq C_{0}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}.

Moreover thanks to Proposition
3.3
and the fact that
\mathbf{P}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})=0
from (
1.12
), we get, for some constant
C_{1}>0
independent of
\varepsilon
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{X}}^{2},

where we have used Lemma
4.1
or Lemma
4.2
in the second line together with
\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\min\{\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}},\|\phi\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\}
. Gathering previous estimates yields

(4.11)

\|\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq C_{0}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+C_{1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{X}}^{2}.

Moreover for
f^{\varepsilon},g^{\varepsilon}\in\mathscr{X}
we first observe that

\begin{aligned} \Phi[f^{\varepsilon}](t)-\Phi[g^{\varepsilon}](t)&=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\left\{\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))-\Gamma(g^{\varepsilon}(\tau),g^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\right\}\,\mathrm{d}\tau.\end{aligned}.

Introducing the symmetrized version
\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}
of
\Gamma
, namely

(4.12)

\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f,g)=\frac{1}{2}\Gamma(f,g)+\frac{1}{2}\Gamma(g,f),

we remark that, arguing as from obtaining the collision invariants in (
1.5
), we have that
\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f,g)
also verifies (
1.12
), which means
\mathbf{P}\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f,g)=0
.
We therefore obtain

(4.13)

\displaystyle\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}](t)-\Phi[g^{\varepsilon}](t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)-g^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(g^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)-g^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau

with
\mathbf{P}\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})=0
and
\mathbf{P}\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(g^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})=0
.
Hence Proposition
3.3
and Lemma
4.1
or Lemma
4.2
yields

\displaystyle\|\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]-\Phi[g^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(g^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon},g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle+\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

thus we get, for some constant
C_{1}>0
independent of
\varepsilon
,

(4.14)

\displaystyle\|\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]-\Phi[g^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq C_{1}(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{X}}+\|g^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{X}})\|f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{X}}.

As a consequence of estimates (
4.11
)–(
4.14
) we can construct a global solution
f^{\varepsilon}\in\mathscr{X}
to the equation (
4.1
) if
\eta_{0}>0
is small enough. Indeed let
B_{\mathscr{X}}(\eta)=\{f\in\mathscr{X}\mid\|f\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq\eta\}
for
\eta>0
be the closed ball in
\mathscr{X}
of radius
\eta
. Choose

\eta=2C_{0}\eta_{0}\quad\text{and}\quad\eta_{0}\leq\frac{1}{8C_{0}C_{1}},

and observe that
\eta_{0}
does not depend on
\varepsilon
. Then for any
f^{\varepsilon}\in B_{\mathscr{X}}(\eta)
we have from (
4.11
) that

\|\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq 2C_{0}\eta_{0}=\eta,

and for any
f^{\varepsilon},g^{\varepsilon}\in B_{\mathscr{X}}(\eta)
we have from (
4.14
) that

\displaystyle\|\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]-\Phi[g^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{0}C_{1}\eta_{0}\|f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\|f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{X}}.

Thus
\Phi:B_{\mathscr{X}}(\eta)\to B_{\mathscr{X}}(\eta)
is a contraction and therefore there is a unique
f^{\varepsilon}\in B_{\mathscr{X}}(\eta)
such that
\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]=f^{\varepsilon}
, which is then a solution to (
4.1
).
This completes the proof of global existence in Theorem
2.1
–(1) together with estimate (
2.10
).

4.2.2.
Uniqueness

Consider two solutions
f^{\varepsilon},g^{\varepsilon}\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})\cap L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}))
to (
4.1
) associated to the same initial data
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}))
satisfying
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\leq\eta_{0}
with
\eta_{0}>0
small enough and

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

\displaystyle\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Arguing as in the existence proof above, we obtain

\displaystyle\|f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right)\left(\|f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right).

Using that
\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|g^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\lesssim\eta_{0}
is small enough we conclude the proof of uniqueness in Theorem
2.1
–(1).

4.2.3.
Decay for hard potentials

Let
f^{\varepsilon}
be the solution to (
4.1
) constructed in Theorem
2.1
–(1) associated to the initial data
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}
, and let
\lambda>0
be given by Proposition
3.2
. Using Proposition
3.4
and Proposition
3.5
we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}.

Thanks to Lemma
4.1
we have

\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}\lesssim\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},

therefore using that
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}
from the existence result in Theorem
2.1
–(1), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathrm{e}_{\lambda}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Since
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\leq\eta_{0}
is small enough, the last term in the right-hand side can be absorbed into the left-hand side, which thus concludes the proof of the decay estimate (
2.11
) in Theorem
2.1
–(1).

4.2.4.
Decay for soft potentials

Let
f^{\varepsilon}
be the solution to (
4.1
) constructed in Theorem
2.1
–(1) associated to the initial data
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}
with
\ell>0
, and let
0<\omega<\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}
.

Using Proposition
3.6
and Proposition
3.7
we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}},

and from Lemma
4.1
we have

\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}\lesssim\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}.

Using that
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}
from the existence result in in Theorem
2.1
–(1), we deduce

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\mathrm{p}_{\omega}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Since
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\leq\eta_{0}
is small enough, the last term in the right-hand side can be absorbed into the left-hand side, which thus concludes the proof of the decay estimate (
2.12
) in Theorem
2.1
–(1).

4.3.
Proof of Theorem
2.1
–(2)

We consider the whole space case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
.

4.3.1.
Global existence

Let
\ell=0
in the hard potentials case
\gamma+1s\geq 0
,
\ell\geq 0
in the soft potentials case
\gamma+2s<0
.
Recall that
p\in(3/2,\infty]
and define the space

\displaystyle\mathscr{Y}=\Big\{f\in

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})\cap L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,**}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}))\cap L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})\cap L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,**}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}))\;\Big|\;\|f\|_{\mathscr{Y}}<\infty\Big\},

with, recalling that
\|\cdot\|_{H^{s,**}_{v}}
is defined in (
2.8
),

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathscr{Y}}

\displaystyle:=\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Let
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})\cap L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}))
verify

\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\leq\eta_{0},

and consider the map
\Phi:\mathscr{Y}\to\mathscr{Y}
,
f^{\varepsilon}\mapsto\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]
given by (
4.9
), which in particular satisfies (
4.10
) for all
\xi\in\mathbf{R}^{3}
.

Thanks to Proposition
3.2
we deduce, for some constant
C_{0}>0
independent of
\varepsilon
, that

\|U^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{\mathscr{Y}}\leq C_{0}\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right).

Moreover thanks to Proposition
3.3
and the fact that
\mathbf{P}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})=0
from (
1.12
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{Y}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right)\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

where we have used Lemma
4.1
or Lemma
4.2
in the second line together with
\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\min\{\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}},\|\phi\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\}
. We now observe that, splitting
\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}=\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}+\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}
, on the one hand we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

On the other hand

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-1}\,\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

where we have used Hölder’s inequality in last line, using that
p>3/2
so that
\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-1}\in L^{p^{\prime}}_{\xi}
. Putting together the two last estimates, we have

(4.15)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

We hence deduce that there is some constant
C_{1}>0
, independent of
\varepsilon
, such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\right).

Therefore, gathering previous estimates, we obtain

(4.16)

\|\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\mathscr{Y}}\leq C_{0}\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}\right)+C_{1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathscr{Y}}^{2}.

Moreover, for
f^{\varepsilon},g^{\varepsilon}\in\mathscr{Y}
we first observe that
\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]-\Phi[g^{\varepsilon}]
satisfies (
4.13
). Therefore
we obtain arguing as above, thanks to Proposition
3.3
together with
\mathbf{P}\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})
, Lemma
4.1
and Lemma
4.2
, that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)-g^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

and similarly

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(g^{\varepsilon}(\tau),f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)-g^{\varepsilon}(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(g^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(g^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon},g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon}-g^{\varepsilon},g^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}

\displaystyle+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\|\widehat{g}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Together with (
4.15
) for the terms in
\|\cdot\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}
, this implies that, for some constant
C_{1}>0
independent of
\varepsilon
,

(4.17)

\|\Phi[f^{\varepsilon}]-\Phi[g^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\mathscr{Y}}\leq C_{1}(\|f\|_{\mathscr{Y}}+\|g\|_{\mathscr{Y}})\|f-g\|_{\mathscr{Y}}.

As a consequence of estimates (
4.16
)–(
4.17
) we can construct a global solution
f^{\varepsilon}\in\mathscr{Y}
to the equation (
4.1
) if
\eta_{0}>0
is small enough by arguing as in Section
4.2.1
. This completes the proof of global existence in Theorem
2.1
–(2) together with estimate (
2.13
).

4.3.2.
Uniqueness

Using the above estimates, we can argue as in Section
4.2.2
.

4.3.3.
Decay for hard potentials

Let
f^{\varepsilon}
be the solution to (
4.1
) constructed in Theorem
2.1
–(2) associated to the initial data
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}
, and let
0<\vartheta<\frac{3}{2}(1-\frac{1}{p})
. Arguing as above, using Proposition
3.8
and Proposition
3.9
we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}.

Thanks to Lemma
4.1
we have

\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}\lesssim\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},

and by (
4.15
) we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\widehat{\mathbf{P}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\widehat{\mathbf{P}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}},

where we have used the estimate of Theorem
2.1
–(2) in last line. Observing that we also have
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}
, it follows

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\right).

Since
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\leq\eta_{0}
is small enough, the last term in the right-hand side can be absorbed into the left-hand side, which thus concludes the proof of the decay estimate (
2.14
) in Theorem
2.1
–(2).

4.3.4.
Decay for soft potentials

Let
0<\vartheta<\frac{3}{2}(1-\frac{1}{p})
.
Let
f^{\varepsilon}
be the solution to (
4.1
) constructed in Theorem
2.1
–(2) associated to the initial data
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}
with
\ell>\vartheta|\gamma+2s|
. Arguing as above, using Proposition
3.10
and Proposition
3.11
we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}.

For the nonlinear term above, we argue as in Section
4.3.3
so that

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{\Gamma}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\left(\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\widehat{\mathbf{P}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\widehat{\mathbf{P}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\right).

Therefore, using the estimate of Theorem
2.1
–(2), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}+\left\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\mathrm{p}_{\vartheta}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\right).

Since
\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\leq\eta_{0}
is small enough, the last term in the right-hand side can be absorbed into the left-hand side, which thus concludes the proof of the decay estimate (
2.15
) in Theorem
2.1
–(2).

5.
Well-posedness for the Navier-Stokes-Fourier system

We start by considering the incompressible Navier-Stokes equation, that is, the first equation in (
1.17
).
We denote by
V
the semigroup associated to the operator
\nu_{1}\Delta_{x}
, and we also denote, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

\widehat{V}(t,\xi)=\mathcal{F}_{x}(V(t)\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x})(\xi)=e^{-\nu_{1}|\xi|^{2}t}.

We shall obtain below boundedness and integrated-in-time regularization estimates for
V
as well as for its integral in time against a source
\int_{0}^{t}V(t-\tau)S(\tau)\,\mathrm{d}\tau
.

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
p\in[1,\infty]
. Let
u_{0}\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{p}_{\xi})
and suppose moreover that
u_{0}
is mean-free in the torus case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
.
Then

\displaystyle\|\widehat{V}(\cdot)\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\||\xi|\widehat{V}(\cdot)\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}},

and moreover
V(t)u_{0}
also is mean-free for all
t\geq 0
in the torus case (that is it satisfies (
1.19
)).

Remark 5.1
.

Observe that, in the torus case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
, one can replace
|\xi|\widehat{V}(\cdot)\widehat{u}_{0}
in above estimate by
\langle\xi\rangle\widehat{V}(\cdot)\widehat{u}_{0}
since
V(t)u_{0}
is mean-free.

Proof.

Let
u(t)=V(t)u_{0}
, which satisfies

\partial_{t}u=-\nu_{1}\Delta_{x}u,\quad u_{|t=0}=u_{0}.

We already observe that, in the torus case, the solution
u(t)
is also mean-free, that is satisfies (
1.19
). For all
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
we thus have

\partial_{t}\widehat{u}(t,\xi)=-\nu_{1}|\xi|^{2}\widehat{u}(t,\xi),\quad\widehat{u}(\xi)_{|t=0}=\widehat{u}_{0}(\xi),

thus for any
t\geq 0
we have

|\widehat{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|\xi|^{2}|\widehat{u}(\tau,\xi)|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim|\widehat{u}_{0}(\xi)|^{2}.

Taking the supremum in time and then taking the square-root of previous estimate yields

\|\widehat{u}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\||\xi|\widehat{u}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim|\widehat{u}_{0}(\xi)|,

and we conclude the proof by taking the
L^{p}_{\xi}
norm.
∎

Proposition 5.2
.

Suppose
p\in[1,\infty]
. Let
S=S(t,\xi)
satisfies
\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\widehat{S}\in L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}
and (
1.19
) in the torus case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
, and

|\xi|^{-1}\widehat{S}\in L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}
in the whole space case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
. Denote

u_{S}(t)=\int_{0}^{t}V(t-\tau)S(\tau)\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Then in the torus case we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{u}_{S}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle\widehat{u}_{S}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\widehat{S}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}.

and in the whole space case

\displaystyle\|\widehat{u}_{S}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\||\xi|\widehat{u}_{S}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{S}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}.

Proof.

We first observe that
u_{S}
satisfies

\partial_{t}u_{S}+\nu_{1}\Delta_{x}u_{S}=S,\quad{u_{S}}_{|t=0}=0.

We only prove the whole space case, the case of the torus being similar by observing that
u_{S}
is mean-free, that is verifies (
1.19
).

For all
\xi\in\mathbf{R}^{3}
and all
t\geq 0
we have

\partial_{t}\widehat{u}_{S}(t,\xi)+\nu_{1}|\xi|^{2}\widehat{u}_{S}(t,\xi)=\widehat{S}(t,\xi),\quad\widehat{u_{S}(\xi)}_{|t=0}=0.

We can compute

\partial_{t}\frac{1}{2}|\widehat{u}_{S}(t,\xi)|^{2}+\nu_{1}|\xi|^{2}|\widehat{u}_{S}(\xi)|^{2}\leq(\widehat{S}(\xi),\widehat{u}_{S}(\xi)),

which implies, for all
t\geq 0
,

|\widehat{u}_{S}(t,\xi)|^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|\xi|^{2}|\widehat{u}_{S}(\tau,\xi)|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}||\xi|^{-1}S(\tau,\xi)|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Taking the supremum in time, then taking the square-root of the estimate, and taking the
L^{P}_{\xi}
norm, the proof is thus finished.
∎

We now obtain bilinear estimates for the operator
Q_{\mathrm{NS}}
defined in (
1.16
).

Lemma 5.3
.

Let
p\in[1,\infty]
. Let
u,v\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}\cap L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t})
, then

(5.1)

\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(v,u)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\|v\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\|u\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}},

and also

(5.2)

\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(v,u)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\|v\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}\|u\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}.

Proof.

From the definition of
Q_{\mathrm{NS}}
, we first observe that for all
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
and
j\in\{1,2,3\}
we have

\displaystyle\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(v,u)^{j}(\xi)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\mathrm{i}\xi_{k}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{x}(v^{j}u^{k})(\xi)-\frac{1}{|\xi|^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{3}\xi_{j}\xi_{l}\mathcal{F}_{x}(v^{l}u^{k})(\xi)\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\mathrm{i}\xi_{k}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{x}(u^{j}v^{k})(\xi)-\frac{1}{|\xi|^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{3}\xi_{j}\xi_{l}\mathcal{F}_{x}(u^{l}v^{k})(\xi)\right\}.

We obtain

|\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(v,u)(\xi)|\lesssim|\xi|\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}|\widehat{v}(\eta)||\widehat{u}(\xi-\eta)|\,\mathrm{d}\eta,

thus by Minkowski’s inequality and then Hölder’s inequality

\displaystyle\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(v,u)(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}|\widehat{v}(t,\eta)|^{2}|\widehat{u}(t,\xi-\eta)|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\right)^{1/2}\mathrm{d}\eta

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{v}(\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\|\widehat{u}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta.

We then conclude the proof of (
5.1
) by taking the
L^{p}_{\xi}
norm above and applying Young’s convolution inequality. The proof of (
5.2
) can be obtained in a similar way, by exchanging the role of
u
and
v
when applying Hölder’s inequality with respect to the time variable.
∎

5.1.
Global existence in the torus
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}

We shall construct mild solutions to the first equation in (
1.17
), namely

(5.3)

u(t)=V(t)u_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}V(t-\tau)Q_{\mathrm{NS}}(u(\tau),u(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

We define the space

\mathscr{X}=\left\{u\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}\cap L^{1}_{\xi}(\langle\xi\rangle)L^{2}_{t})\mid u\text{ satisfies }\eqref{eq:meanfree},\;\|u\|_{\mathscr{X}}<\infty\right\},

with

\|u\|_{\mathscr{X}}:=\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}.

Let
u_{0}\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi})
be mean-free and

\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}\leq\eta_{1}.

Consider the map
\Phi:\mathscr{X}\to\mathscr{X}
,
u\mapsto\Phi[u]
defined by, for all
t\geq 0
,

(5.4)

\Phi[u](t)=V(t)u_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}V(t-\tau)Q_{\mathrm{NS}}(u(\tau),u(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

thus, for all
\xi\in\mathbf{Z}^{3}
,

(5.5)

\widehat{\Phi}[u](t,\xi)=\widehat{V}(t,\xi)\widehat{u}_{0}(\xi)+\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{V}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u(\tau),u(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

For the first term we have from Proposition
5.1
that

\|\widehat{V}(t,\xi)\widehat{u}_{0}(\xi)\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq C_{0}\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}},

and by Proposition
5.2
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{V}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u(\tau),u(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u,u)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle\xi\rangle\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{\mathscr{X}}^{2},

where we have used Lemma
5.3
. Thus we obtain

\|\Phi[u]\|_{\mathscr{X}}\lesssim C_{0}\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+C_{1}\|u\|_{\mathscr{X}}^{2}.

Moreover for
u,v\in\mathscr{X}
we can also compute, using again Proposition
5.2
and Lemma
5.3
, that

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{V}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}((u-v)(\tau),v(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}+\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{V}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u(\tau),(u-v)(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u-v,v)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u,u-v)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}\|\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}.

Therefore there is
C_{1}>0
such that

\|\Phi[u]-\Phi[v]\|_{\mathscr{X}}\leq C_{1}(\|u\|_{\mathscr{X}}+\|v\|_{\mathscr{X}})\|u-v\|_{\mathscr{X}}.

Gathering the two inequalities and arguing as in Sections
4.2.1
and
4.2.2
, we can construct a global unique solution
u\in\mathscr{X}
to the equation (
5.3
) if
\eta_{1}>0
is small enough, which moreover satisfies

\|u\|_{\mathscr{X}}\lesssim\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}.

Once
u
have been constructed, we can then argue in a similar and even simpler way in order to construct a global unique mild solution
\theta
for the second equations in (
1.17
) if
\eta_{1}>0
is small enough, namely

\theta(t)=\overline{V}(t)\theta_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\overline{V}(t-\tau)\left[-\Div_{x}(u(\tau)\theta(\tau))\right]\mathrm{d}\tau,

where
\overline{V}
denotes the semigroup associated to the operator
\nu_{2}\Delta_{x}
, and which satisfies moreover

\|\theta\|_{\mathscr{X}}\lesssim\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}.

We finally obtain the solution
\rho
by using the last equation in (
1.17
) and observing that we consider mean-free solutions, so that
\widehat{\rho}(t,0)=\widehat{\theta}(t,0)=0
. This completes the proof of Theorem
2.2
–(1).

5.2.
Global existence in the whole space
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}

Similarly as before we define the space, recalling that
p\in(3/2,+\infty]
,

\mathscr{Y}=\left\{u\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}\cap L^{1}_{\xi}(|\xi|)L^{2}_{t})\cap\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}\cap L^{p}_{\xi}(|\xi|)L^{2}_{t})\mid\|u\|_{\mathscr{Y}}<\infty\right\},

with
