\|u\|_{\mathscr{Y}}:=\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\||\xi|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\||\xi|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}.

Let
u_{0}\in\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{x}(L^{1}_{\xi}\cap L^{p}_{\xi})
satisfy

\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}}\leq\eta_{1},

and consider the map
\Phi:\mathscr{Y}\to\mathscr{Y}
,
u\mapsto\Phi[u]
defined by (
5.4
), in particular (
5.5
) is verified for all
\xi\in\mathbf{R}^{3}
.

For the first term in (
5.5
) we have from Proposition
5.1
that

\|\widehat{V}(t,\xi)\widehat{u}_{0}(\xi)\|_{\mathscr{Y}}\leq C_{0}(\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}}).

Furthermore, by Proposition
5.2
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{V}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u(\tau),u(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{Y}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u,u)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u,u)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\left(\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}\right).

where we have used Lemma
5.3
. We now observe that

\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}},

and for the first term we easily have

\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\||\xi|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}.

For the second term we use Hölder’s inequality to obtain

\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-1}\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{\xi}}\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\||\xi|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}},

where we have used that
\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-1}\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{\xi}}<\infty
since
p>3/2
. Therefore we get

(5.6)

\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\||\xi|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\||\xi|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}.

Gathering previous estimates, we have hence obtained

\|\Phi[u]\|_{\mathscr{Y}}\leq C_{0}\left(\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\|\widehat{u}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}}\right)+C_{1}\|u\|_{\mathscr{Y}}^{2}.

Moreover for
u,v\in\mathscr{Y}
we can also compute, using again Proposition
5.2
and Lemma
5.3
, that

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{V}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}((u-v)(\tau),v(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}+\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{V}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u(\tau),(u-v)(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right\|_{\mathscr{X}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u-v,v)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u,u-v)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u-v,v)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\||\xi|^{-1}\widehat{Q}_{\mathrm{NS}}(u,u-v)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}\|\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}\|\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\left(\|\widehat{u}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}+\|\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}}\right)\left(\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}+\|\widehat{u}-\widehat{v}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}\right).

Using inequality (
5.6
) we therefore get, for some constant
C_{1}>0
,

\|\Phi[u]-\Phi[v]\|_{\mathscr{Y}}\leq C_{1}\left(\|u\|_{\mathscr{Y}}+\|v\|_{\mathscr{Y}}\right)\|u-v\|_{\mathscr{Y}}.

Gathering these two inequalities together, the proof of Theorem
2.2
–(2) is completed by arguing as in Section
5.1
above.

6.
Hydrodynamic limit

Recalling that the semigroup
U^{\varepsilon}
is defined in (
3.8
), and also
\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}
in (
3.7
), we also define, for all
t\geq 0
,

(6.1)

\Psi^{\varepsilon}[f,g](t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}U^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f(\tau),g(\tau))\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

as well as its Fourier transform in space, for all
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

(6.2)

\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[f,g](t,\xi)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{\Gamma}_{\mathrm{sym}}(f(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

where we recall that
\Gamma_{\mathrm{sym}}(f,g)
is the symmetrized version of
\Gamma(f,g)
defined in (
4.12
).

6.1.
Estimates on
\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}

We denote that
0\leq\chi\leq 1
is a fixed compactly supported function of
B_{1}
equal to one on
B_{\frac{1}{2}}
, where
B_{R}
is the ball with radius
R
centered at zero.

Arguing as in
[
13
,
39
]
but using the spectral estimates of
[
75
,
76
]
for the non-cutoff Boltzmann equation, we then have:

Lemma 6.1
.

There exist
\kappa>0
such that one can write

U^{\varepsilon}(t)=\sum_{j=1}^{4}U_{j}^{\varepsilon}(t)+U^{\varepsilon\#}(t),

with

\widehat{U}_{j}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi):=\widehat{U}_{j}(\frac{t}{\varepsilon^{2}},\varepsilon\xi),\quad\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)=\widehat{U}^{\#}(\frac{t}{\varepsilon^{2}},\varepsilon\xi),

where we have the following properties:

(1)

For
1\leq j\leq 4
,

\widehat{U}_{j}(t,\xi)=\chi(\frac{|\xi|}{\kappa})e^{t\lambda_{j}(\xi)}P_{j}(\xi),

with
\lambda_{j}
satisfying

\lambda_{j}(\xi)=i\alpha_{j}(\xi)-\beta_{j}|\xi|^{2}+\gamma_{j}(|\xi|),

with

\alpha_{1}>0,\quad\alpha_{2}<0,\quad\alpha_{3}=\alpha_{4}=0,\quad\beta_{j}>0,

and

\gamma_{j}(|\xi|)=O(|\xi|^{3}),\quad\hbox{as}\quad\xi\to 0,\quad\gamma_{j}(\xi)\leq\beta_{j}|\xi|^{2}/2,\quad\forall|\xi|\leq\kappa,

as well as

P_{j}(\xi)=P_{j}^{0}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})+|\xi|P_{j}^{1}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})+|\xi|^{2}P_{j}^{2}(\xi),

with
P_{j}^{n}
bounded linear operators on
L^{2}_{v}
with operator norms uniformly bounded for
|\xi|\leq\kappa
.

(2)

We also have that the orthogonal projector
\mathbf{P}
onto
\Ker L
satisfies

\mathbf{P}=\sum_{j=1}^{4}P_{j}^{0}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|}).

Moreover
P_{j}^{0}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})
,
P_{j}^{1}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})
and
P_{j}^{2}(\xi)
are bounded from
L^{2}_{v}
to
L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{l})
uniformly in
|\xi|\leq\kappa
for all
l\geq 0
.

(3)

In the hard potentials case
\gamma+2s\geq 0
, for all
t\geq 0
and all
\xi\in\mathbf{R}^{3}
there holds, for any
\ell\geq 0
,

(6.3)

\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\leq Ce^{-\lambda_{1}\frac{t}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})},

for any
f
satisfying moreover (
1.13
) in the torus case, where
\lambda_{1},C>0
are independent of
t,\xi,\varepsilon
.

(4)

In the soft potential case
\gamma+2s<0
, for all
t\geq 0
and all
\xi\in\mathbf{R}^{3}
there holds, for any
k,\ell\geq 0
,

(6.4)

\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{k})}\leq C\left(1+\frac{t}{\varepsilon^{2}}\right)^{-\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{k+\ell})},

for any
f
satisfying moreover (
1.13
) in the torus case, where
C>0
is independent of
t,\xi,\varepsilon
.

Proof.

The proof is the same as in
[
24
, Lemma 5.10]
. For the soft potentials case, we need to replace the use of
[
75
, Theorem 3.2 and Remark 5.2]
in the proof by
[
76
, Theorem 1.1 and Section 4]
, in particular the decay estimate (
6.4
) comes from
[
76
, Equation (2.46)]
and the fact that
B_{0}(\xi)\mathbf{P}^{\perp}=B(\xi)\mathbf{P}^{\perp}
, where
B_{0}(\xi)
and
B(\xi)
are defined in
[
76
, Equation (1.18)]
and satisfy
B_{0}(\xi)=B(\xi)-\mathbf{P}
.
∎

Denoting

\widetilde{P}_{j}\left(\xi,\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\right):=P^{1}_{j}\left(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\right)+\xi P^{2}_{j}(\xi),

for
1\leq j\leq 4
, we can further split
\widehat{U}_{j}^{\varepsilon}
into four parts (one main part and three remainder terms):

(6.5)

U_{j}^{\varepsilon}=U_{j0}^{\varepsilon}+U_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}+U_{j1}^{\varepsilon}+U_{j2}^{\varepsilon},

where

\displaystyle\widehat{U}_{j0}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)=e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}P_{j}^{0}\left(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{U}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)=\left(\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)-1\right)e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}P_{j}^{0}\left(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{U}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)=\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}\left(e^{t\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}-1\right)P_{j}^{0}\left(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{U}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)=\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}e^{t\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\varepsilon|\xi|\widetilde{P}_{j}\left(\varepsilon\xi,\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\right).

In particular we observe that
\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}_{30}
and
\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}_{40}
are independent of
\varepsilon
, so that we define

(6.6)

\widehat{U}(t,\xi):=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}_{30}(t,\xi)+\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}_{40}(t,\xi),

which is then independent of
\varepsilon
. We finally define

(6.7)

U(t)=\mathcal{F}_{x}^{-1}\widehat{U}(t)\mathcal{F}_{x}.

We say that a function
f=f(x,v)\in\Ker L
is well-prepared if

f(x,v)=\left\{\rho[f](x)+u[f](x)\cdot v+\theta[f](x)\frac{(|v|^{2}-3)}{2}\right\}\sqrt{\mu}(v)

with

\nabla_{x}\cdot u[f]=0\quad\text{and}\quad\rho[f]+\theta[f]=0,

where we recall that
\rho[f],u[f],\theta[f]
are defined in (
2.4
)

Lemma 6.2
.

(
[
39
]
, Proposition A.3)
We have that
U(0)
is the projection on the subset of
\Ker L
consisting of functions
f
that are well-prepared.
We also have

U(t)f=U(t)U(0)f,\quad\forall t\geq 0,

and

\nabla_{x}\cdot u[f]=0\quad\hbox{and}\quad\rho[f]+\theta[f]=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad P_{j}^{0}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})f=0,\quad j=1,2.

The following lemma studies the limit of
U^{\varepsilon}(t)
as
\varepsilon
goes to 0.

Lemma 6.3
.

Let
f=f(x,v)\in\Ker L
be well-prepared, then we have

\|(\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)-\widehat{U}(\cdot))\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}},

and

\|(\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)-\widehat{U}(\cdot))\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\||\xi|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}.

Proof.

The proof follows the idea of
[
39
, Lemma 3.5]
, that we shall adapt since we work in different functional spaces.

First of all we observe that from the decomposition of
U^{\varepsilon}
in (
6.5
) we can write, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

\displaystyle\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)-\widehat{U}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\left\{\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}_{j0}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)+\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}_{j1}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)+\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}_{j2}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}_{j0}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)+\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi),

and we shall estimate each term separately below.

We first compute the term
U_{jm}^{\varepsilon}(t)f
for
j=1,2,3,4
and
m=1,2
. For the
U_{j1}
term, using Lemma
6.1
together with the inequality
|e^{a}-1|\leq|a|e^{|a|}
for any
a\in\mathbf{R}^{+}
, we have

(6.8)

\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)e^{-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}\left|e^{t\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}-1\right|\leq\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)e^{-\frac{\beta_{j}}{2}t|\xi|^{2}}t\varepsilon|\xi|^{3}\lesssim\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\varepsilon|\xi|\lesssim\min\{1,\varepsilon|\xi|\}.

Then we can compute, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\mathbf{R}^{3}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{U}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\leq

\displaystyle\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\left|e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}\right|\left(e^{t\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}-1\right)\|P_{j}^{0}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\{1,\varepsilon|\xi|\}\|\hat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}.

For the
\widehat{U}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)
term we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{U}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\leq

\displaystyle\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\left|e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}e^{t\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\right|\varepsilon|\xi|\|\tilde{P}_{j}(\varepsilon\xi,\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\{1,\varepsilon|\xi|\}\|\hat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}.

For the term
\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}_{j0}(t,\xi)
, using the fact that

(6.9)

\left|\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)-1\right|\lesssim\min\{1,\varepsilon|\xi|\},

we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{U}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\leq

\displaystyle\left(\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)-1\right)\left|e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}\right|\|P_{j}^{0}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\{1,\varepsilon|\xi|\}\|\hat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}.

Taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}
norm in both sides yields, for all
j=1,2,3,4
,

\|\widehat{U}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{U}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{U}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}(\cdot)\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\min\{\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}},\varepsilon\||\xi|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\}.

By Lemma
6.2
we have, if
f\in\Ker L
is a well-prepared data, then

U^{\varepsilon}_{10}f+U^{\varepsilon}_{20}f=0.

Finally we compute the term
U^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)
, noticing that

\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi,v)=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(0,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi,v)=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\left(1-\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{4}P_{j}(\varepsilon\xi)\right)\widehat{f}(\xi,v).

Since
f
belongs to
\Ker L
, we have

\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi,v)=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\left(1-\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)-\varepsilon|\xi|\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{4}\tilde{P}_{j}(\varepsilon\xi)\right)\widehat{f}(\xi,v).

By Proposition
3.2
we deduce

\displaystyle\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(\cdot)\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\Big(1-\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)-\varepsilon|\xi|\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{4}\tilde{P}_{j}(\varepsilon\xi)\Big)\widehat{f}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\{\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}},\varepsilon\||\xi|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\},

thus the proof is finished by gathering together the two previous estimates.
∎

6.2.
Estimates on
\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}

The decomposition of the semigroup
U^{\varepsilon}(t)
in (
6.5
) also gives us a decomposition of the operator
\Psi^{\varepsilon}(t)
defined in (
6.1
).

Lemma 6.4
.

The following decomposition holds

\Psi^{\varepsilon}=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\Psi_{j}^{\varepsilon}+\Psi^{\varepsilon\#},

with

\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}[f_{1},f_{2}](t,\xi):=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{\Gamma}_{\mathrm{sym}}(f_{1}(\tau),f_{2}(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

and, for all
1\leq j\leq 4
,

\Psi_{j}^{\varepsilon}=\Psi_{j0}^{\varepsilon}+\Psi_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}+\Psi_{j1}^{\varepsilon}+\Psi_{j2}^{\varepsilon},

where

\displaystyle\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon}[f_{1},f_{2}](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|P_{j}^{1}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}_{\mathrm{sym}}(f_{1}(\tau),f_{2}(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

\displaystyle\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}[f_{1},f_{2}](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\left(\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)-1\right)\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|P_{j}^{1}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}_{\mathrm{sym}}(f_{1}(\tau),f_{2}(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

\displaystyle\widehat{\Psi}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}[f_{1},f_{2}](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\left(e^{(t-\tau)\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}-1\right)|\xi|P_{j}^{1}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}_{\mathrm{sym}}(f_{1}(\tau),f_{2}(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

\displaystyle\widehat{\Psi}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}[f_{1},f_{2}](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}e^{(t-\tau)\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\varepsilon|\xi|^{2}P_{j}^{2}(\varepsilon\xi)\widehat{\Gamma}_{\mathrm{sym}}(f_{1}(\tau),f_{2}(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Similarly as above, we observe again that that
\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}_{30}
and
\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}_{40}
are independent of
\varepsilon
, so that we define

(6.10)

\widehat{\Psi}[f,g](t,\xi):=\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}_{30}[f,g](t,\xi)+\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}_{40}[f,g](t,\xi)

which is then independent of
\varepsilon
. We finally define

(6.11)

\Psi[f,g](t)=\mathcal{F}_{x}^{-1}\widehat{\Psi}[f,g](t)\mathcal{F}_{x}.

We are now able to prove the following result on the convergence of
\Psi^{\varepsilon}
towards
\Psi
.

Lemma 6.5
.

Let
(\rho_{0},u_{0},\theta_{0})
satisfy the hypotheses of Theorem
2.2
and consider the associated global unique solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
to (
1.17
). Let also
g_{0}=g_{0}(x,v)\in\Ker L
be defined by (
2.16
) and
g=g(t,x,v)\in\Ker L
by (
2.18
). Then we have:

(1)

Torus case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
: There holds

\|\Psi^{\varepsilon}[g,g]-\Psi[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}\right).

(2)

Whole space case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
: For any
p\in(3/2,\infty]
there holds

\|\Psi^{\varepsilon}[g,g]-\Psi[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}\right).

Proof.

We adapt the proof of
[
39
, Lemma 4.1]
for the cutoff Boltzmann equation with hard potentials.
Thanks to the decomposition of
\Psi^{\varepsilon}
in Lemma
6.4
we write, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,\xi)-\widehat{\Psi}[g,g](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\left\{\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}_{j0}[g,g](t,\xi)+\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}_{j1}[g,g](t,\xi)+\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}_{j2}[g,g](t,\xi)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}_{j0}[g,g](t,\xi)+\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g](t,\xi).

We remark that for the zero frequency
\xi=0
we have

\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,0)=\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g](t,0).

We split the proof into several steps and estimate each term separately below.

Step 1.

By Lemma
6.4
and (
6.9
), for the term
\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g]
with
j=1,2,3,4
, for all
t\geq 0
and all
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}\setminus\{0\}
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g](t,\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)-1\right|\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|\left\|P_{j}^{1}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|^{2}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Similarly for the term
\widehat{\Psi}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]
, by Lemma
6.4
and (
6.8
) we have, for all
j=1,2,3,4
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\left|e^{(t-\tau)\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}-1\right||\xi|\left\|P_{j}^{1}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\beta_{j}}{4}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|^{2}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Similarly for the term
\widehat{\Psi}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]
, by Lemma
6.4
we have, for all
j=1,2,3,4
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\chi\left(\frac{\varepsilon|\xi|}{\kappa}\right)\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\left|e^{(t-\tau)\frac{\gamma_{j}(\varepsilon|\xi|)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\right|\varepsilon|\xi|^{2}\left\|P_{j}^{2}(\varepsilon\xi)\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\beta_{j}}{4}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|^{2}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}
norm on both side we finally obtain, for all
j=1,2,3,4
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Thanks to
[
69
]
and the fact that
\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\mathbf{P}\phi\|_{H^{m}_{v}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}}
for all
m,\ell\geq 0
, we have

(6.12)

\|\Gamma(\mathbf{P}g_{1},\mathbf{P}g_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}g_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbf{P}g_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{v}},

therefore arguing as in Lemma
4.1
it follows, for any
p\in[1,\infty]
and
\ell\geq 0
,

(6.13)

\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\lesssim\|g\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|g\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

We therefore obtain, for all
j=1,2,3,4
,

(6.14)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j1}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{\Psi}_{j2}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|g\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.

Step 2.

We now focus on the term
\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]
with
j=1,2
, and recall that
\alpha_{j}>0
for
j=1,2
. We denote

\widehat{H_{j}}(t,\tau,\xi)=e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|P_{j}^{1}(\tfrac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi),

and thus, using integration by parts, for all
t\geq 0
and all
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}\setminus\{0\}
we have

(6.15)

\displaystyle\widehat{\Psi}_{j0}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|P_{j}^{1}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{\varepsilon}{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|}\left(\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}}\partial_{\tau}\widehat{H_{j}}(t,\tau,\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau-\widehat{H}_{j}(t,t,\xi)+e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}}\widehat{H_{j}}(t,0,\xi)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\varepsilon}{i\alpha_{j}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}}\beta_{j}|\xi|^{2}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}P_{j}^{1}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon}{i\alpha_{j}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t-\tau}{\varepsilon}}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}P_{j}^{1}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\partial_{\tau}\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\varepsilon}{i\alpha_{j}}P_{j}^{1}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}(g(t),g(t))(\xi)

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon}{i\alpha_{j}}e^{i\alpha_{j}|\xi|\frac{t}{\varepsilon}}e^{-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}P_{j}^{1}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\widehat{\Gamma}(g(0),g(0))(\xi)

\displaystyle=:I_{1}(t,\xi)+I_{2}(t,\xi)+I_{3}(t,\xi)+I_{4}(t,\xi).

For the first term in (
6.15
) we have for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
, using Lemma
6.4
,

\displaystyle\|I_{1}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}\beta_{j}|\xi|^{2}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Similarly, for the third term in (
6.15
) there holds

\displaystyle\|I_{3}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(t),g(t))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

and for the fourth one

\displaystyle\|I_{4}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\beta_{j}t|\xi|^{2}}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(0),g(0))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

This yields

(6.16)

\|I_{1}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|I_{3}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|I_{4}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}^{2},

where we have used (
6.13
) in last inequality.

For the second term in (
6.15
) we first write, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

\displaystyle\|I_{2}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\|\partial_{\tau}\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Since
\partial_{\tau}\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)=\widehat{\Gamma}(\partial_{\tau}g,g)+\widehat{\Gamma}(g,\partial_{\tau}g)
, from (
6.12
) we get

\|\partial_{\tau}\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial_{\tau}g(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta.

As
g
is defined through
(u,\theta,\rho)
which satisfies the Navier-Stokes-Fourier system (
1.17
), we have for all
\tau\geq 0
and all
\eta\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}

\|\partial_{\tau}\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim|\eta|^{2}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+|\eta|\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta.

This implies

\displaystyle\|I_{2}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\eta|^{2}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\eta|\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta\,\mathrm{d}\eta\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle=:R_{1}(t,\xi)+R_{2}(t,\xi).

For the term
R_{1}
we split the integral on
\eta
into two parts: the region
2|\xi|>|\eta|
in which we have
|\eta|^{2}\leq 4|\xi|^{2}
; and the region
2|\xi|\leq|\eta|
where we have
|\eta-\xi|\sim|\eta|
, which yields

\displaystyle R_{1}(t,\xi)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\mathbf{1}_{|\eta|<2|\xi|}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\eta|^{2}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\mathbf{1}_{|\eta|\geq 2|\xi|}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\eta|^{2}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}|\xi|^{2}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}|\xi-\eta|\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\eta|\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

Thanks to Hölder’s inequality in the time variable, it follows

\displaystyle\|R_{1}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}|\xi-\eta|\|\widehat{g}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}|\eta|\|\widehat{g}(\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta,

therefore taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}
norm and using Young’s convolution inequality we obtain

(6.17)

\displaystyle\|R_{1}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\varepsilon\||\xi|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.

For the term
R_{2}
we write

\displaystyle\|R_{2}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{0}^{t}|\xi|e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}|\xi|^{-1}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\eta|\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta\,\mathrm{d}\eta\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\sup_{t\geq 0}\left(\int_{0}^{t}|\xi|^{2}e^{-\beta_{j}(t-\tau)|\xi|^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right)^{1/2}|\xi|^{-1}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}G(\tau,\xi)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right)^{1/2},

where we denote

G(\tau,\xi)=\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}H(\tau,\eta)\,\mathrm{d}\eta,\quad H(\tau,\eta)=|\eta|\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta.

By Minkowski and Hölder inequalities

\displaystyle\|G(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}|H(\tau,\eta)|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right)^{1/2}\mathrm{d}\eta

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\|\widehat{g}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|H(\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\,\mathrm{d}\eta.

Moreover

H(\tau,\eta)\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}|\eta-\zeta|\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta+\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\zeta|\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta.

Thus again by Minkowski and Hölder inequalities,

\displaystyle\|H(\eta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}\||\eta-\zeta|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right)^{1/2}\mathrm{d}\zeta

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{g}(\tau,\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\||\zeta|\widehat{g}(\tau,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right)^{1/2}\mathrm{d}\zeta

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\||\zeta|\widehat{g}(s,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta.

Hence we get

\displaystyle\|R_{2}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon|\xi|^{-1}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\||\zeta|\widehat{g}(\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta\,\mathrm{d}\eta.

Taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}
norm and distinguishing between high and low frequencies yields

(6.18)

\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|\geq 1}R_{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\||\xi|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

and, in the whole space case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
and
\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
,

(6.19)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}R_{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-1}\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{\xi}}\left\|\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\eta}}\int_{\Omega^{\prime}_{\zeta}}\|\widehat{g}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}(\eta-\zeta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\||\zeta|\widehat{g}(\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\zeta\,\mathrm{d}\eta\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\||\xi|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

where we have used that
\mathbf{1}_{|\xi|<1}|\xi|^{-1}\in L^{p^{\prime}}_{\xi}
since
p>3/2
.

Step 3.

It only remains to compute the term
\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}
, for which we first write, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g](t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon\#}(t-\tau,\xi)\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau.

In the hard potentials case
\gamma+2s\geq 0
, thanks to (
6.3
) we have, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g](t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{1}\frac{(t-\tau)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{1}\frac{(t-\tau)}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

For the soft potentials case
\gamma+2s<0
, observing that
\mathbf{P}\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)=0
we fix
\ell>0
such that
\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}>1
then we use (
6.4
) to obtain, for all
t\geq 0
and
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g](t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{(t-\tau)}{\varepsilon^{2}}\right)^{-\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g(\tau),g(\tau))(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{(t-\tau)}{\varepsilon^{2}}\right)^{-\frac{\ell}{|\gamma+2s|}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{\Gamma}(g,g)(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell})}.

Taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}
norm in above estimates and using (
6.13
) yields, for both hard potentials and soft potentials cases,

(6.20)

\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon\#}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.

Step 4: Conclusion.
We conclude the proof by gathering estimates (
6.14
), (
6.16
), (
6.17
), (
6.18
), (
6.19
), and (
6.20
) together with the bounds for
g
from Theorem
2.2
.
∎

6.3.
Proof of Theorem
2.3

Let
f^{\varepsilon}
, for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
, be the unique global mild solution to (
1.9
) associated to the initial data
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}
constructed in Theorem
2.1
.

Let
g=\mathbf{P}g
be the kinetic distribution defined by (
2.18
) through the unique global mild solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
to (
1.17
) associated to the initial data
(\rho_{0},u_{0},\theta_{0})
constructed in Theorem
2.2
, and denote also
g_{0}=\mathbf{P}g_{0}
the initial kinetic distribution defined by (
2.16
) through the initial data
(\rho_{0},u_{0},\theta_{0})
.

We now from
[
13
,
39
]
for instance, that
g
verifies the equation

(6.21)

g(t)=U(t)g_{0}+\Psi[g,g](t),

where we recall that
U(t)
is defined in (
6.7
), and
\Psi(t)
in (
6.11
). Taking the Fourier transform in
x\in\Omega_{x}
, we then have

(6.22)

\widehat{g}(t,\xi)=\widehat{U}(t,\xi)\widehat{g}_{0}(\xi)+\widehat{\Psi}[g,g](t,\xi).

for all
\xi\in\Omega^{\prime}_{\xi}
, and where we recall that
\widehat{U}
is defined in (
6.6
), and
\widehat{\Psi}
in (
6.10
).

We first observe that the difference
f^{\varepsilon}-g
satisfies

(6.23)

\displaystyle\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}(\xi)-\widehat{g}(\xi)

\displaystyle=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}(\xi)-\widehat{U}(t,\xi)\widehat{g}_{0}(\xi)+\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}](t,\xi)-\widehat{\Psi}[g,g](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)\left\{\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}(\xi)-\widehat{g}_{0}(\xi)\right\}+\left\{\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(t,\xi)-\widehat{U}(t,\xi)\right\}\widehat{g}_{0}(\xi)

\displaystyle+\left\{\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,\xi)-\widehat{\Psi}[g,g](t,\xi)\right\}+\left\{\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}](t,\xi)-\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,\xi)\right\}

\displaystyle=:T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4},

and we estimate each one of these terms separately.

For the first term, from Lemma
6.1
we have

\|\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)\{\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}-\widehat{g}_{0}\}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}-\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}.

Thanks to Lemma
6.3
and an interpolation argument, we obtain for the second term, for any
\delta\in[0,1]
,

\|\{\widehat{U}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot)-\widehat{U}(\cdot)\}\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\delta}\|\langle\xi\rangle^{\delta}\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}.

For the third term we use Lemma
6.5
, which yields

\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]-\widehat{\Psi}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}\right),

in the case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
, and

\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[g,g]-\widehat{\Psi}[g,g]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}\right),

in the case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
.

For the fourth term
T_{4}
, we first decompose
f^{\varepsilon}=\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}+\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}
and use that
g=\mathbf{P}g
to write

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}](t,\xi)-\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[g,g](t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}](t,\xi)+2\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}](t,\xi)

\displaystyle+\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g)](t,\xi)+\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}g,\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g)](t,\xi).

Thanks to Proposition
3.3
and Lemma
4.1
we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{\Gamma}[\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},

moreover

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{\Gamma}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\widehat{\Gamma}[\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}},

where we have used that
\|\mathbf{P}\phi\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}}
and
\|\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma/2+s)_{-}}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\min\{\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}},\|\phi\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\}
.
This implies

(6.24)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+2\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbf{P}^{\perp}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}H^{s,*}_{v}}.

Therefore, using the bounds of Theorem
2.1
, we deduce from (
6.24
) that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+2\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2},

in the case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
, and

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+2\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}^{\perp}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right),

in the case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
.

Furthermore, from Proposition
3.3
and Lemma
4.1
, and also using that
\|\mathbf{P}\phi\|_{H^{s,*}_{v}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{v}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g)]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{\Gamma}(\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g),\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\widehat{\Gamma}(\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g))\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}(\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

and similarly

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}g,\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g)]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{\Gamma}(\mathbf{P}g,\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g))\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}+\|\widehat{\Gamma}(\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g),\mathbf{P}g)\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}(H^{s,*}_{v})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}(\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g})\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\|\mathbf{P}\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

In the case of the torus
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
, we can use the bounds of Theorem
2.1
–(1) and Theorem
2.2
–(1) to obtain

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g)]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}g,\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g)]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\right)\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

In the case of the whole space
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
, we first use (
4.15
) to write

\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{v}},

then we use the bounds of Theorem
2.1
–(2) and Theorem
2.2
–(2) to get

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g),\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{\Psi}^{\varepsilon}[\mathbf{P}g,\mathbf{P}(f^{\varepsilon}-g)]\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}\right)\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}.

Gathering previous estimates and using that
\eta_{2}>0
is small enough, so that when taking the
L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}
norm of (
6.23
) the fourth and fifth terms in the right-hand side of (
6.23
) can be absorbed by the left-hand side, we deduce

(6.25)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}-\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\varepsilon^{\delta}\|\langle\xi\rangle^{\delta}\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}\right)+\varepsilon\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

in the case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{T}^{3}
, and

(6.26)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}-\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}+\varepsilon^{\delta}\|\langle\xi\rangle^{\delta}\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{g}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{3}\right)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\left(\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)

in the case
\Omega_{x}=\mathbf{R}^{3}
.
From these estimates, we first conclude that

\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0}\|\widehat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\widehat{g}\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{v}}=0,

assuming moreover that
\langle\xi\rangle^{\delta}\widehat{g}_{0}\in L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}
for some
\delta\in(0,1]
. We can finally prove Theorem
2.3
, where we only assume
\widehat{g}_{0}\in L^{1}_{\xi}L^{2}_{v}
, by using the previous convergence and arguing by density as in
[
24
]
. This completes the proof of Theorem
2.3
.
